Para workflows Python com múltiplos bancos de dados, prefira bioservices. Use isto para trabalho HTTP/REST direto ou controle específico de KEGG. - **kegg-database**: 当需查 KEGG 通路/基因/化合物/药物、在 KEGG↔NCBI/UniProt/PubChem 转 ID、查药物相互作用或取通路富集背景时使用；requests 直连 REST API（七操作）产出基因集与 FASTA/MOL/PNG。不适用于多库 Python 聚合（用 gget-genomic-databases）。触发词：KEGG、通路、pathway、ID 转换、富集 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/kegg-database) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **kegg-database**: Retrieve KEGG entries, pathways, links, sequences, maps, or identifier conversions with the documented REST API, respecting provider access terms, request rate, and operation-specific limits. ID conversion (NCBI, UniProt, PubChem). For Python workflows with multiple databases, prefer bioservices. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/kegg-database) · ★1)

- **kegg-database-matching**: Use when starting an mWISE annotation run on unannotated LC-MS peak lists (m/z features) and you need KEGG compound candidates before applying the cluster-based filter and diffusion scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-database-matching))
- **kegg-database-oimiragieo-agent-studio**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/api/kegg-database-oimiragieo-agent-studio) · ★745)
- **kegg-database-ovachiever-droid-tings**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/api/kegg-database-ovachiever-droid-tings) · ★745)
- **kegg-database-querying**: Use when you have identified two or more organisms (via their KEGG organism codes, e.g., 'hsa' for Homo sapiens) and need to retrieve their complete metabolic pathway and reaction datasets as a prerequisite for network reconstruction or comparative metabolic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/kegg-database-querying))
- **kegg-database-querying**: Use when an interaction table or node list uses identifiers from a source database (e.g. BRENDA EC numbers like 2.7.1.1) that must be converted to KEGG reaction IDs (rn:RXXXXX) or KEGG gene IDs before integrating with KEGG pathway maps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/kegg-database-querying))
- **kegg-enrichment**: 针对马铃薯 DMv8.2、DMv8.1、DMv6.1 和 E4-63 基因组执行 KEGG pathway 富集分析；智能体读取输入前 10 个非空 ID 判断基因组版本，并调用 scripts/KEGG_enrichment.py 完成 ID 转代表转录本、KEGG 富集和图表输出。 ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/kegg-enrichment) · ★1)
- **kegg-functional-annotation-mapping**: Use when shotgun metagenome reads have been mapped to a non-redundant gene catalog (e.g. IGC) and per-sample gene frequencies must be translated into KO, EC number and reaction level functional abundance tables for case-control biomarker analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-functional-annotation-mapping))
- **kegg-gene-search**: Search KEGG database for gene information to retrieve pathway associations, functional annotations, and disease links. ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kegg-gene-search) · ★399 · 3 copies)
- **kegg-gene-to-modelseed-reaction-mapping**: Use when the input is an annotated amino acid FASTA (Type 1) and the goal is a KEGG-annotation-based draft GENRE whose reactions are traceable to genes via ModelSEED reaction IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-gene-to-modelseed-reaction-mapping))
- **kegg-go-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a trained single-cell graph-embedding autoencoder (e.g., scMGCA on 'Qx Limb Muscle' or PDAC scRNA-seq data) and need to interpret its compressed transcriptomic space by identifying biological functions of its top pivotal genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-go-pathway-enrichment-analysis))
- **kegg-identifier-based-compound-classification**: Use when a metabolomics count data frame contains KEGG compound identifiers and each feature needs hierarchical class/subclass metadata attached before statistical testing or figure faceting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-identifier-based-compound-classification))
- **kegg-identifier-to-mass-mapping**: Use when you have raw LC-MS peak intensity data with mass-to-charge ratios and need to match them to known metabolites. This skill must be applied before the matching stage if you are working with a KEGG database (KeggDB or sample.keggDB) and require a precomputed adduct/fragment lookup table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/kegg-identifier-to-mass-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **kegg-kgml-pathway-data-retrieval**: Use when the goal is to automatically reconstruct a metabolic network for one or two KEGG organisms from KEGG database information, requiring organism-specific KGML downloads and parsing of compounds and reactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-kgml-pathway-data-retrieval))
- **kegg-kgml-reaction-extraction**: Use when building the functional level of a KEGG-based metabolic network for an organism: you need, per reference pathway, the set or multiset of reactions (and compounds) that MetNet compares between two organisms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-kgml-reaction-extraction))
- **kegg-kgml-xml-parsing**: Use when KEGG KGML pathway XMLs must be inspected or rewritten in Cytoscape so that multi-gene nodes (functional homolog / OR groups) show all responsible genes, not just the first listed gene that KEGGScape renders by default. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-kgml-xml-parsing))
- **kegg-metabolite-database-searching**: Use when you have preprocessed LC/MS feature tables (m/z per feature, e.g. from apLCMS cdf.to.ftr or XCMS centWave) and need to quantify how many features can be matched to known metabolites via KEGG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-metabolite-database-searching))
- **kegg-module-abundance-quantification**: Use when PICRUSt-predicted KO metagenome tables from 16S data must be aggregated to KEGG module abundances and statistically compared between sample groups (e.g., supragingival vs subgingival plaque, menses vs rest of cycle). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-module-abundance-quantification))
- **kegg-module-annotation-mapping**: Use when a large phylogeny (thousands of taxa, e.g. PhyloXML) must be annotated per leaf with KEGG module presence/absence or genome-size metadata as external rings in a circular cladogram. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-module-annotation-mapping))
- **kegg-module-block-partitioning-comparison**: Use when evaluating microbial genome or MAG functional potential via KEGG Modules and needing to compare block-level Module completeness across KEMET, KEGG Mapper, and METABOLIC v4.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-module-block-partitioning-comparison))
- **kegg-module-brite-functional-category-mapping**: Use when predicted or observed gene family annotations (KOs, COGs) must be aggregated into higher-level functional categories via KEGG Modules or BRITE, or when per-category accuracy of gene-content predictions is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-module-brite-functional-category-mapping))
- **kegg-module-completeness-evaluation**: Use when you have a FASTA genome/MAG plus a KEGG KO annotation file (eggNOG/kaas/kofamkoala format) and need Module-level completeness tables or a comparison of Module block partitioning across KEMET, KEGG Mapper, and METABOLIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-module-completeness-evaluation))
- **kegg-module-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you have a full (unprefiltered) KO/compound abundance table plus sample metadata with grouping, and need threshold-free, multi-group or longitudinal KEGG pathway/module enrichment rather than single-list over-representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-module-enrichment-interpretation))
- **kegg-network-node-name-mapping**: Use when KGML pathway XMLs have been expanded to show all functionally homologous genes per node and node names must be unified (e.g., as 'PLA2G4B-B21') before mapping node-level differential-expression values in Cytoscape. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-network-node-name-mapping))
- **kegg-network-topology-parsing**: Use when a rule-inference or dynamic-modeling pipeline needs pathway topology scaffolds and the source is KEGG (organism code + pathway IDs), or when non-KEGG networks must be loaded in GRAPHML format for downstream Boolean rule inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-network-topology-parsing))
- **kegg-organism-network-structural-comparison**: Use when you have reconstructed the two-level metabolic networks of two KEGG organisms and need to compare their structural level — pathway connectivity through shared non-ubiquitous compounds — pathway by pathway or globally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-organism-network-structural-comparison))
- **kegg-ortholog-annotation-removal**: Use when you need to quantitatively validate an annotation-expansion tool (e.g., KEMET) by removing known KOs from eggNOG-mapper .emapper.annotations files and measuring recovery rates from FASTA sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-ortholog-annotation-removal))
- **kegg-ortholog-ec-reaction-enrichment-analysis**: Use when mapping-based metagenomic count data (case-control design) has genes mapped to KEGG KO groups, EC numbers, and reactions, and you need to decide whether functional-level differential analysis outperforms gene-level testing at a fixed significance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-ortholog-ec-reaction-enrichment-analysis))
- **kegg-ortholog-functional-annotation**: Use when raw paired-end metagenome reads must be converted into per-sample KEGG ortholog (KO) abundance profiles using predicted operon sequences as the alignment reference, e.g. for disease-vs-control pathway association testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-ortholog-functional-annotation))
- **kegg-ortholog-hierarchical-rendering**: Use when you have a HUMAnN-derived KEGG functional profile matrix plus a LEfSe biomarker result and need a publication-quality circular functional tree highlighting pathways enriched in contrasting sample cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-ortholog-hierarchical-rendering))
- **kegg-ortholog-hmm-search**: Use when a MAG or genome FASTA has incomplete KEGG Module blocks and unannotated genes that may be genuine KOs recoverable by profile HMM searches, especially for partial/truncated gene sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-ortholog-hmm-search))
- **kegg-ortholog-pathway-aggregation**: Use when you have a corrected KO abundance table from shotgun metagenomes and need higher-level KEGG pathway/module functional profiles rather than ortholog-level abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-ortholog-pathway-aggregation))
- **kegg-ortholog-to-reaction-mapping**: Use when a MAG or isolate genome has KEGG KO annotations (eggNOG/KAAS/KofamKOALA format) and you need to convert them into metabolic reactions for GSMM reconstruction or evidence-based gapfilling, especially after HMM-based KO recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-ortholog-to-reaction-mapping))
- **kegg-orthology-annotation**: Use when you have a per-sample gene abundance table and a many-to-many gene-to-KEGG-KO (or other ortholog) mapping file, and need a KO-level functional profile without double-counting multiply-annotated genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-orthology-annotation))
- **kegg-orthology-annotation-of-mapped-genes**: Use when a non-redundant gene catalog has already been read-mapped to produce per-sample gene frequencies, and the analysis goal is functional profiling beyond the gene level at KO, EC number and reaction levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-orthology-annotation-of-mapped-genes))
- **kegg-orthology-annotation-processing**: Use when you already have KO-annotated genes for a genome or MAG in eggnog/kaas/kofamkoala format and need KEGG Module completeness summaries, or need the KEGG-Mapper INCOMPLETE convention (Modules with &lt;3 blocks) for cross-tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-orthology-annotation-processing))
- **kegg-orthology-functional-annotation**: Use when raw metagenomic FASTQ reads must be converted into functional feature tables, specifically KEGG orthology (KO) abundance tables alongside taxonomic profiles, per the Liu2020ProteinCell reproducible pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/kegg-orthology-functional-annotation))
- **kegg-pathway**: Query the KEGG REST API for metabolic pathways, genes, compounds, drugs, and diseases. Use when the user needs pathway mapping, gene-to-pathway links, compound info, or cross-reference ID conversion. NOT for protein sequences (use UniProt), 3D structures (use PDB), or variant/SNP data (use NCBI). ([beita6969/ScienceClaw](https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/kegg-pathway) · ★1k · 2 copies)
- **kegg-pathway-abundance-estimation**: Use when the only input is 16S rDNA amplicon data (QIIME 2 feature table, BIOM/TSV plus representative sequences) and the research goal is to estimate metagenomic functional composition such as KEGG pathway abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-abundance-estimation))
- **kegg-pathway-abundance-profiling**: Use when you have raw fecal shallow shotgun metagenomics sequencing data (e.g. the n=666 3xTg/WT Sacrifice cohort deposit on Qiita) and need per-sample KEGG pathway abundances plus genotype-level enrichment/depletion calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-abundance-profiling))
- **kegg-pathway-accessibility-quantification**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics experiment (e.g. MetaboLights MTBLS136 split by assay type) needs its assay-specific chemical bias quantified as a count or proportion of KEGG human pathways reachable for ORA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-accessibility-quantification))
- **kegg-pathway-adjacency-construction**: Use when a KEGG organism code is available and the goal is a graph-based (topological) representation of its metabolism — pathways as nodes, shared-compound relations as edges — for later comparison or visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-adjacency-construction))
- **kegg-pathway-analysis**: Guide to KEGG pathway enrichment for DEG results. Covers ORA vs GSEA, mandatory directionality splitting, KEGG organism codes, API failure handling with offline fallbacks, cross-condition comparisons, and answer-first reporting. Consult when running enrichment with clusterProfiler or gseapy. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/systems-biology-multiomics/kegg-pathway-analysis) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **kegg-pathway-analysis**: Guide to KEGG pathway enrichment analysis for differential expression results. Covers over-representation analysis (ORA) vs gene set enrichment analysis (GSEA), mandatory directionality splitting, KEGG organism codes, API failure handling with offline fallbacks, cross-condition pathway comparisons, and answer-first reporting. Consult this when running pathway enrichment with clusterProfiler or gseapy on DEG results. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/kegg-pathway-analysis) · ★1)
- **kegg-pathway-annotation**: Use when a ggpicrust2 differential abundance (DA) results table carries KO-level or raw KEGG pathway identifiers and needs pathway_name, pathway_description, pathway_class, and pathway_map joined from the KEGG database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-annotation))
- **kegg-pathway-annotation-mapping**: Use when a time-series gene expression analysis needs pathway-level interpretation of TF–target networks, and KEGG/Reactome/GO gene-set files must be joined to genes and TFs to identify pathway–regulator interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-annotation-mapping))
- **kegg-pathway-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a ranked protein–protein association list (e.g. FAVA latent-space Pearson correlations or favapy output) must be validated or calibrated against a known pathway benchmark such as KEGG co-membership. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-benchmark-evaluation))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a metabolite list (KEGG compound IDs converted via MetaboAnalyst ID Conversion) must be mapped to KEGG pathways to find where the selected metabolites occur and ranked by occurrence count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--5a05011626c7aaeb))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when standalone metabolomics data must be linked to disease/phenotype GWAS results by testing whether metabolite-interacting genes from KEGG pathways are over-represented in GWAS gene networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--20eee06cf54e57a9))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when raw and imputed versions of an scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., the adam h5 AD matrix vs GraphSCI/scGCL-imputed versions) must be compared at the DEG/pathway level rather than by clustering metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--bba7fe47fff860cf))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a table of DEGs (up- and down-regulated lists, e.g. from non-model species like salamander) needs to be tested for significantly enriched KEGG/GO biological functions via ORA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--dd2d5284512b6bdd))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have ranked gene lists (e.g., SSrGE rank_genes() output) from multiple scRNA-seq datasets and need to test which KEGG pathways are over-represented among top-ranked genes and combine evidence across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--46a6f4cb9b0bc144))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a BUSseq fit has yielded a ranked intrinsic-gene list (e.g., 1,419 genes from hematopoietic scRNA-seq data at Bayesian FDR &lt; 0.05) and biological interpretability must be assessed via KEGG pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--a3dcd7b60fc4b8f6))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a filtered differential network edge list (e.g. top ~1000 TF-filtered BoostDiff edges) with Louvain module assignments and need to test whether module/network genes are enriched in disease-relevant KEGG pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-analysis--db0245b3a5594482))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you have a deduplicated list of gene IDs linked to a TF's binding regions (e.g., from a Region Query of ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks) and need pathway-level interpretation such as KEGG disease pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when a differential metabolomics experiment (e.g., control vs mutant lung cancer) yields a metabolite list or ranked statistics and you must identify significantly enriched KEGG pathways and prioritize them by biological impact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation))
- **kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when a filtered gene set with gene symbols (e.g., 208 high-confidence ASE genes with global ASE score &gt;= 50%) needs functional interpretation, and PPI/KEGG enrichment statistics must be reported from STRING. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-enrichment-interpretation))
- **kegg-pathway-graph-construction**: Use when preparing pathway-level kernel association tests (e.g., PaIRKAT) on metabolomics data where each metabolite must be assigned to a KEGG pathway graph with reaction-derived topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-graph-construction))
- **kegg-pathway-id-resolution**: Use when a metabolomics feature table contains non-standard identifiers (e.g., RI-coded names from GC retention time) that must be converted to standard compound IDs before KEGG pathway enrichment in MeTEor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-id-resolution))
- **kegg-pathway-interpretation**: Use when a list of essential genes from SKO/DKO in silico knockout of context-specific metabolic models must be functionally interpreted via KEGG enrichment with a custom background and ferroptosis annotation via FerrDb. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-interpretation))
- **kegg-pathway-kgml-retrieval**: Use when building a stacked 2.5D trans-omic network in Cytoscape whose layers are KEGG pathways (e.g. insulin signaling rno04910/mmu04910 over metabolic maps rno01100/mmu01100), requiring KGML retrieval and ID conversion to KEGG-compatible IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-kgml-retrieval))
- **kegg-pathway-library-construction**: Use when rebuilding or refreshing metabolomics pathway-analysis libraries from KEGG, or when verifying reported library counts (e.g. ~130 species, ~3700 RaMP-DB pathways) after a knowledgebase update. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-library-construction))
- **kegg-pathway-map-url-generation**: Use when a metabolomics workflow has converted compound identifiers (e.g. HMDB IDs) to KEGG IDs via MetaboAnalyst ID Conversion and needs ranked pathway enrichment plus interactive pathway-map links highlighting the query metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-map-url-generation))
- **kegg-pathway-mapping**: Use when a table of significant metabolites carrying KEGG compound IDs must be associated with KEGG pathways for a chosen species and ranked by enrichment p-value, as in the MetENP ST000915 workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-mapping--4a3b3c6919e1e5d0))
- **kegg-pathway-mapping**: Use when an annotated feature table from LC-MS/MS metabolomics (e.g., GNPS2 FBMN with ChemWalker/SIRIUS annotations) needs KEGG compound-to-pathway assignment and cross-strain pathway comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-mapping--f9b80eb25458bd26))
- **kegg-pathway-metabolite-retrieval**: Use when MetaboAnalyst outputs ('name_map.csv' and 'pathway_results.csv') are available and KEGG pathways with FDR ≤ 0.05 must be populated with their member metabolites annotated as HMDB IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-metabolite-retrieval))
- **kegg-pathway-network-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq data must be analyzed in the context of KEGG signaling pathway networks, or when node importance scores need to be compared against network centrality measures to test topology-dependence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-network-analysis))
- **kegg-pathway-network-mapping**: Use when building species-specific metabolic networks from KEGG layouts and NCBI-RefSeq annotations and refining them with empirical metabolomics data to flag missing or disconnected reactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-network-mapping))
- **kegg-pathway-node-expansion**: Use when KEGG pathway visualization in Cytoscape/KEGGScape hides homologous genes (only the first listed gene per reaction node is shown) and RNA-seq read counts are available to prioritize the dominant enzyme per node. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-node-expansion))
- **kegg-pathway-overrepresentation-analysis**: Use when patient- or condition-specific inferred networks must be partitioned into functional modules and tested for KEGG pathway over-representation to reproduce cluster-level pathway frequency analyses (e.g., Fig. 4D Ras-module style extraction). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-overrepresentation-analysis))
- **kegg-pathway-prediction-from-spectra**: Use when you have untargeted MS2 spectral data (in MS2MP-compatible format) and need to assign KEGG pathway annotations to unknown metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-prediction-from-spectra) · 2 copies)
- **kegg-pathway-set-construction**: Use when you have PICRUSt2 KO abundance features and need pathway-level gene sets (e.g. for ggpicrust2 pathway_gsea()) rather than single-feature differential abundance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-set-construction))
- **kegg-pathway-set-enrichment-of-lr-pairs**: Use when you have factor loadings for ligand-receptor pairs from a Tensor-cell2cell decomposition (e.g., the 6 ASD factors) and need to identify which KEGG-derived biological pathways each factor is enriched for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kegg-pathway-set-enrichment-of-lr-pairs))
- **kegg-pathway-set-filtering**: Use when assembling pathway sets from KEGG (or Reactome/BioCyc) as ORA input for metabolomics data, to exclude pathways with fewer than 3 compounds from the over-representation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-set-filtering))
- **kegg-pathway-set-property-characterization**: Use when deciding between the KEGG organism-specific human compound-only pathway set (261 pathways, mean size 30) and the KEGG reference compound-only set (377 pathways, mean size 45) for ORA on metabolomics data, and you need quantitative evidence of how much more/larger pathways in one set drive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-set-property-characterization))
- **kegg-pathway-similarity-index-computation**: Use when two KEGG organisms' metabolic networks have been reconstructed from KGML files and you need quantitative pairwise comparison scores (SimPi, SimSi, Psim, PsimW, Csim) in [0,1]. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-similarity-index-computation))
- **kegg-pathway-topology-retrieval**: Use when a multi-omics (gene + metabolite) pathway analysis needs the underlying KEGG pathway graph topology — node types, compound/gene identifiers, and gene–metabolite interactions — as input, e.g. to run eSPIA in the SMART 2.0 IOPA module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-topology-retrieval))
- **kegg-pathway-visualization**: Use when a DESeq2 differential expression table or ranked gene list has produced significant KEGG pathways via ClusterProfShinyORA or ClusterProfShinyGSEA and the next step is to visualize those enriched pathways as KEGG pathway plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-pathway-visualization))
- **kegg-pathway-visualization-overlay**: Use when a metabolomics analysis needs a visual, pathway-level rendering of metabolite abundance changes on the canonical KEGG diagram after metabolites have been mapped to KEGG pathway IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-pathway-visualization-overlay))

- **kegg-query**: Query KEGG database for drug information, pathway analysis, and disease-drug-target discovery. Use this skill when: (1) Looking up drug information including efficacy, targets, metabolism, and interactions, (2) Analyzing metabolic or signaling pathways to retrieve genes, compounds, and modules, (3) Discovering disease-associated drugs, genes, and pathways for drug repurposing. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/kegg-query) · ★1.1k)
- **kegg-rclass-reaction-pair-retrieval**: Use when reconstructing the KMRN layer of KGMN or any metabolic reaction network that needs curated known metabolite nodes and reactant-pair reaction edges from KEGG RCLASS as a knowledge seed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-rclass-reaction-pair-retrieval))
- **kegg-reaction-gap-tabulation**: Use when a metabolomics-driven network-refinement study reports reaction gaps against a reference metabolic database (e.g. KEGG) and these must be normalized into a per-reaction table with pathway class, species, and literature-support flags. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-reaction-gap-tabulation))
- **kegg-reaction-gene-retrieval**: Use when a metabolite enrichment table (e.g. MetENP met_path output for a study like ST000915) has associated KEGG pathways and you need the underlying genes, reactions, EC enzymes, and each metabolite's reactant/product role. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-reaction-gene-retrieval))
- **kegg-reaction-network-mapping**: Use when an untargeted LC-MS/MS peak table (MS1 m/z, RT, MS2 spectra) has seed 'known' peaks matched against a standard spectral library and you must annotate reaction-paired unknown peaks via a KEGG-style metabolic reaction network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-reaction-network-mapping))
- **kegg-reference-database-mapping**: Use when DA result tables from ggpicrust2::pathway_daa() carry KO-level or unannotated feature IDs and need KEGG pathway names, descriptions, and classes joined on before visualization or reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kegg-reference-database-mapping))
- **kegg-rest-api-querying**: Use when a pathway-metabolite association table (met_path) already exists and you need gene IDs, EC numbers, reaction IDs, and reactant/product roles for the metabolites in those pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-rest-api-querying))
- **kegg-rpair-edge-derivation**: Use when building biochemical (substrate-product) reaction networks from a metabolite identifier list and the identifiers may be common database names or synonyms rather than KEGG compound IDs, with no pathway annotation required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-rpair-edge-derivation))
- **kegg-smpdb-pathway-set-enrichment**: Use when you have sex-stratified (or group-stratified) differential metabolite tables and need to identify which biological pathways are enriched in one group versus another, mirroring the ferroptosis/bile-acid (male) and glutathione/cAMP/cGMP-PKG (female) CRC results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kegg-smpdb-pathway-set-enrichment))
- **kegg_drug**: &gt; Approved drugs — structures, targets, pathways & drug-drug interactions &gt; **Category:** Drug-centric | **Type:** DB | **Subcategory:** DDI &gt; **API:** `https://rest.kegg.jp` (free, no key required for academic use) ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/drugclaw/kegg_drug) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **keggrest-api-queries**: Use when a metabolomics workflow yields compound IDs that must be linked to KEGG pathways and visualized on interactive pathway maps — e.g., the pathway enrichment step of MeTEor after metabolite ID conversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/keggrest-api-queries))
- **kei**: Kei 言語のコードを学習なしで正しく書くための取扱説明書。func/契約/エフェクト/型/失敗処理の最小十分セットと頻出エラーの直し方を一括ロードする。Kei の .kei ファイルを生成・編集するときに読む。 ([A-1ro/kei-lang](https://github.com/A-1ro/kei-lang/tree/HEAD/skills/kei))
- **kei**: Role-play as ケイ of Blue Archive. ([ncaq/blue-prompt](https://github.com/ncaq/blue-prompt/tree/HEAD/plugins/role-play/skills/kei))
- **kei-agents**: Use the kei-agents package for agent definitions, tools, and schemas on the Kei AI-assistant platform. Use when defining agent capabilities, tool schemas, permission gates, multi-model tool rendering (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, Llama, vLLM), governed connector read schemas (GitHub, Linear, Google Drive/Docs, S3, http_api/CRM), policy/permission evaluation, or semantic mappings between tools and connectors. Covers render_tools, ModelFormat, Permission, ToolDefinition, ToolBinding, PolicyEngine, and validate_tool_definitions; reads are connector capabilities, writes are agent action tools. ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-agents) · ★4)
- **kei-api**: Use governed Kei API contracts for organizations, workspaces, data connectors, groups, policies, users, invitations, agents, access levels, roles, consents, and audit when no supported kei CLI command exists. Use for API/resource design, contract verification, or current API-only surfaces; prefer the kei CLI for any released resource command. Covers auth schemes, AIP/CRUD conventions, workspace scope, and endpoint ownership. ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-api) · ★4)
- **kei-api-conventions**: Define or change a Kei HTTP endpoint following the resource-oriented (Google AIP-style) conventions adopted in ADR-019. Use when adding a route to kei-policy-catalog, renaming an endpoint, designing a custom method, adding pagination to a list endpoint, or when the "Endpoint conventions" CI check fails. Covers collection naming, the :verb custom-method spelling, the dual-write migration from legacy paths, and the aipcheck baseline. Do not use for connector capability definitions (kei-agents) or for calling existing endpoints (kei-api). ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-api-conventions) · ★4)
- **kei-audit-encryption**: Set up and use opt-in audit-args encryption with the kei CLI: generate a customer-held age (X25519) identity locally, register only the public key so the runtime encrypts audit tool-call arguments to it, verify records carry args_ciphertext, decrypt a record locally to a file, rotate keys (add new, disable old), and manage the escrow setting. Use when someone asks to encrypt audit arguments or tool-call payloads, why a record has no args_ciphertext, how to recover or decrypt audit args, how to rotate, back up, or disable an audit encryption key, what is and is not encrypted in Kei audit recor… ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-audit-encryption) · ★4)
- **kei-cli**: The `kei` platform-administration CLI for Kei (run by an org owner/admin, not by agents) — install/upgrade, login, runtime installations, agent moves, workspaces, groups, users, service principals, directory sync, policies (`kei policy`, with deprecated `kei policies` alias), connectors, harness management, model profiles, credential store, and Haikei skill installation for supported harnesses (`kei connect claude-code|codex|opencode|pi|cursor`). Load before running or suggesting any `kei` command. Policy sources are groups or `autogroup:member` / `autogroup:admin`; direct access uses ID-base… ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-cli) · ★4)
- **kei-code-review**: Kei コンパイラ(この Rust ワークスペース)の PR / ブランチ diff を、Kei 固有不変条件と過去 PR 教訓に押さえてレビューする。組み込み /code-review よりトークン消費が抑えられる(角度を Kei 向け 5 個に圧縮 + verify 前 dedup)。`--comment` で findings を GitHub PR の inline comment として投稿。「Kei レビューして」「PR#NN を Kei 用にレビュー」と言われたら呼ぶ。 ([A-1ro/kei-lang](https://github.com/A-1ro/kei-lang/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kei-code-review))
- **kei-credential-rotation**: Rotate or revoke a Kei runtime installation credential (the KEI_RUNTIME_TOKEN a kei-proxy runtime authenticates with) without leaking it and with a planned cutover, using `kei bot credential --rotate` or the console's Rotate button. Use whenever someone wants to rotate, re-mint, replace, roll, or revoke a Kei runtime token or installation credential, suspects one leaked, sees a bootstrap without workspace_id, or asks how key rotation works in Kei — including when they just say "rotate the Kei keys". ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-credential-rotation) · ★4)
- **kei-directory-sync**: Set up, run and troubleshoot Kei's per-org Entra (Azure AD) directory sync, which turns a customer's Entra app roles into Kei groups and creates their users as org members. Use when a customer admin or an agent helping one wants to sync Entra or Azure AD groups or users into Kei; configure app roles, the sync app registration, Graph permissions or the federated credential (system:serviceaccount:kei:oidc-bridge, api://AzureADTokenExchange); run `kei directory-sync get|set|enable|disable|report`; read a dry-run report; go live; or debug no groups synced, users skipped, a token or AADSTS error,… ([HaikeiLabs/skills](https://github.com/HaikeiLabs/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kei-directory-sync) · ★4)
- **kei-dogfood**: Kei プラグイン / MCP サーバーだけを頼りに、Kei 文法を一切教えず Sonnet サブエージェント(初見ユーザー想定)に .kei を書かせて、取説(SKILL.md / spec / examples)と Diagnostic が「初見で使えるか」を実証する。完了後、ハマりどころ・不足情報を構造化フィードバックとして回収する。「ドッグフード」「初見テスト」「kei MCP の取説テスト」と言われたら呼ぶ。 ([A-1ro/kei-lang](https://github.com/A-1ro/kei-lang/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kei-dogfood))

- **kelin-delegate**: 把边界清楚的单次任务委托给外部 CLI（codex、antigravity、zcode 无头），带回结构化结果并能续接追问；按角色记忆交给谁（review 审查、critique 挑刺、diagnose 会诊、implement 实现），读类角色强制只读沙箱。用户说“交给 codex 审”“让另一个模型看看”“双模型会诊”“委托实现”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-delegate) · ★1)
- **kelin-docs**: 从 skills 目录生成文档：skill 索引、命令速查、一页 agent digest，并做 drift check，手改生成文件会被 CI 拦。用户说“更新文档索引”“生成 agents digest”“文档和 skill 对不上”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-docs) · ★1)
- **kelin-evals**: 给 skill 自己做验收：静态检查结构、描述触发词、引用文件和长度；用真实语料测“该不该触发”；决定什么时候用 hook、什么时候用 AGENTS.md 和 CI。用户说“skill 有没有测过”“加个 eval”“这条规则要不要做成 hook”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-evals) · ★1)
- **kelin-goal**: 把一句模糊的“做成 X”变成可判定的目标契约：完成条件、约束、检查点、谁拍板。也用于“我想跑 goal 但说不清要什么”：先给草稿，只问三个问题，写好 goal 和 plan，再给出可粘贴的 /goal 命令。用户说“定个目标”“跑 goal”“这次要做到什么程度”“验收标准是什么”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-goal) · ★1)
- **kelin-grill**: 把方案、决定或想法一层层问透，只问不答：把决策画成设计树，每轮把“现在就能问的”一次问完、每题给推荐答案，事实自己去查、决定交给他，问到没有悬空分支才收。用户说“拷问我”“grill me”“pressure-test 这个方案”“把这事想透”“别急着写先问清楚”“我是不是漏了什么”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-grill) · ★1)
- **kelin-guard**: 发现一个 bug 后，选择最强且最便宜的防再犯手段并证明它真的能拦住：回归测试优先，其次类型/schema、lint 规则、CI 检查，最后才是 AGENTS.md 一句话。用户说“这个 bug 怎么防再犯”“写个 lint”“加个检查”“别再犯了”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-guard) · ★1)
- **kelin-health**: 质量面板：一次跑完单元测试、结构检查、skill eval、文档漂移、AGENTS 预算和 guards，按检查项报通过/失败，只报不改。用户说“仓库健康吗”“整体检查一下”“质量面板”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-health) · ★1)
- **kelin-legacy**: 老仓库改造：先建立可重复的基线（能不能构建/测试），再画功能地图和热点，补特征测试，按切片做绞杀式改造，每片都能独立验证和回滚。用户说“老项目改造”“接手一个祖传仓库”“迁移/重构”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-legacy) · ★1)
- **kelin-loop**: 有明确停止条件的循环执行：每轮跑验证、记录证据、判断继续还是停，设预算上限和升级条件，禁止无限重试。用户说“循环跑”“自动迭代”“跑到通过为止”“hillclimb”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-loop) · ★1)
- **kelin-map**: 给代码库建立“功能地图”：哪些路径归哪个功能、每个功能怎么验证。产出机器可读的 feature-map.json 和人读的 feature-map.md，让“改哪测哪”可计算。用户说“功能地图”“改哪要测什么”“建个 path 到功能的映射”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-map) · ★1)
- **kelin-mode**: 自动编排整套 kelin-stack：安装后 SessionStart 自动启用；非平凡任务自动进入，按阶段调用 goal、plan（含设计草图）、map、tdd、verify、review、refine、ship，每一步有产物和退出条件。多代理最多 2 个，适配 24GB 内存。用户输入 /kelin-mode，或说“全自动做完这个需求”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-mode) · ★1)
- **kelin-multirepo**: 多仓库老项目统筹：先画仓库与数据所有权地图，再选集成方式（API/事件优先，避免跨仓共享库表），用契约和部署顺序管理跨仓改动。用户说“多个仓库”“仓库太多太乱”“数据共库还是 API”“跨仓改造”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-multirepo) · ★1)
- **kelin-neat**: 收尾时把仓库里所有说法对齐一次：README、AGENTS.md、docs、旧计划、功能地图、验收清单互相矛盾或过期的地方，用代码和配置为准逐个校正，一个事实只留一个家。用户说“整理文档”“文档和代码对不上”“收尾同步一下”“清掉旧 plan”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-neat) · ★1)
- **kelin-plan**: 非平凡任务开工前的规划：复述目标与验收标准、摸清现状、给方案和推荐、拆成可验证的单元；跨模块时先出设计草图（类型签名、模块边界、失败模式、双候选交叉评分）。用户说“先规划一下”“设计一下”“设计草图”“怎么拆”“这个怎么做”“先别写代码”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-plan) · ★1)
- **kelin-principles**: kelin-stack 的操作底线，十条。动手前读一遍，尤其是“先证明再说完成”“部署要人点头”“真实数据不入库”。用户说“按原则来”“检查下有没有违反原则”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-principles) · ★1)
- **kelin-product**: 软件与产品设计的底线：先问清用户和环境；需求不发明、待确认最多 3 个；用户旅程写到能演一遍；先定胃口再谈方案；验收要能被外行判定；PRD 只停一个高度；上传防错、AI 结果人工确认；大数据分页；权限审批第一天设计；性能预算写进 PRD。用户说“做个软件/功能”“写 PRD”“需求没说清”“验收怎么定”“权限/上传导出/性能”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-product) · ★1)
- **kelin-recall**: 接手或续做一件事之前，从仓库状态、提交历史、任务板和会话记忆重建“现在在哪、下一步做什么、有什么坑”。用户说“我们做到哪了”“接着上次”“帮我回忆一下”“接手这个项目”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-recall) · ★1)
- **kelin-refine**: 精炼代码：按必要性阶梯删掉不该存在的东西，合并重复，去掉猜测性抽象，只保留代码本身说不出来的注释。用户说“精简一下”“代码太啰嗦”“删掉没用的”“over-engineering 审查”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-refine) · ★1)
- **kelin-reflect**: 任务结束后复盘：哪些判断错了、哪些做法值得重复，把稳定的结论写进对应的地方（AGENTS.md、项目文档、记忆），而不是写一堆没人看的总结。用户说“复盘一下”“总结下经验”“下次别再犯”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-reflect) · ★1)
- **kelin-release**: 自动化发布：先过发布闸门（CI 绿、验证回执、CHANGELOG），再升版本、打 tag、建 GitHub Release。默认只出计划，真正发布必须显式确认。用户说“发版”“打 tag”“做个 release”“版本号怎么升”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-release) · ★1)
- **kelin-review**: 交付前的自审：按 P0-P3 分级找正确性、边界、测试、安全、范围蔓延问题，只报能举例的；门禁改动只问不修，评审回执绑定工作树指纹。用户说“审一下”“review 一下”“交付前检查”“有没有坑”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-review) · ★1)
- **kelin-safety**: 运行时安全钩子：在命令和写入执行前拦下 rm -rf /、强推默认分支、越界编辑等灾难操作；普通递归删除先问，开发目录里的清理放行。和 kelin-guard 不同，这个管“现在这一下”，不管“以后不再犯”。用户说“防误删”“安全钩子”“拦一下危险命令”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-safety) · ★1)
- **kelin-ship**: 按纪律交付：一任务一分支、带前缀的提交、PR 说明写清验证方式、CI 失败先找根因、部署必须等明确授权、合并后清理。用户说“提交”“开个 PR”“合并”“上线/部署”“收尾”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-ship) · ★1)
- **kelin-tdd**: 测试先行的最小纪律：先写会失败的测试并确认失败原因对，再写最小实现让它变绿，最后在测试不变的前提下重构。用户说“TDD”“先写测试”“怎么保证没改坏”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-tdd) · ★1)
- **kelin-test-audit**: 清理低价值测试：找出永远通过、自己和自己比、只存快照、永久跳过、复制夹具的测试，按退役卡逐条评估，默认只报不改。用户说“测试太多太慢”“哪些测试可以删”“测试没在保护行为”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-test-audit) · ★1)
- **kelin-ui**: 界面审查：看渲染结果而不是看代码，检查层级、状态、响应式、可访问性、文案和性能，按 P0-P3 出可修的问题。装了 impeccable 就调用它做深度审查，没装也能用本 skill 的清单。用户说“UI 看看”“界面审查”“可访问性”“响应式有问题”“不好看”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-ui) · ★1)
- **kelin-unslop**: 去掉文字里的 AI 味：套话、三段式、假对立、抽象大词、过度对冲、装饰性 emoji、聊天腔。用于 README、PRD、PR 正文、提交信息、界面文案。用户说“太 AI 了”“说人话”“润色一下”“去掉套话”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-unslop) · ★1)
- **kelin-verify**: 把“做完了”变成可执行的证明：先写验收断言，再跑基线、跑改动、交证据表。用户说“验证一下”“怎么证明能用”“跑个验收”“别只说做好了”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-verify) · ★1)
- **kelin-verify-app**: 给项目生成一套本地验证 skill：启动、体检、驱动真实用户路径、留证据、清理，并维护功能地图。用于“这个项目怎么验收”“给仓库做一套可重复验证”“每次都说能跑但没有证据”的场景。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-verify-app) · ★1)
- **kelin-verify-stack**: 验证一个 ZCode 插件是不是真的能用。先体检环境，再按改动路径算出该测哪些功能，然后真的开无头 ZCode 会话调用 skill、跑插件自带脚本，用机器断言和哈希回执给结论。默认验证 kelin-stack 自己。用户说“验证插件”“跑一遍全流程”“这个 skill 真的能加载吗”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-verify-stack) · ★1)
- **kelin-watch**: PR/CI 值守：轮询检查状态，把失败分成代码、环境、网络、权限四类，只报新出现的问题，并给出该修、该重跑还是该找人。用户说“盯着这个 PR”“CI 挂了看看”“watch 一下”时使用。 ([kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack](https://github.com/kelinpan0524-cell/kelin-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/kelin-watch) · ★1)

- **kellen-copy-coach**: Kellen-style outbound copy critique, rewrite, and strategic coaching for cold email, LinkedIn DMs, cold DMs, follow-up sequences, lead magnets, reply-generation, and offer/message framing. - **kelley-zig-philosophy**: Write Zig in Andrew Kelley's style: explicit ownership, visible control flow, compile-time used for proof or specialization instead of cleverness, and ABI-aware data layout.
